## [0.8.0] — 2026-06-19

### Added

- **Adapter conventions auto-injected into `pipeline/context.md` — Phase 15.2. Phase 15 complete.** When `deploy.adapter` is set in `.devteam/config.yml`, `devteam run` and `devteam stage` (requirements/design/build) now write a deploy-target context block into `pipeline/context.md` before the first agent dispatches. The block is idempotent (written via `upsertSection`), adapter-specific, and removed cleanly when the adapter changes and pipeline state is reset. Two adapter conventions files ship: `core/deploy/gizmos.conventions.md` (language, runtime, required file structure, no-build-step rule, file tracking, secrets model, health check requirements for Gizmos/Cloudflare Workers) and `core/deploy/cloud-run.conventions.md` (runtime, structure, `PORT` env var, state/persistence model, health check requirements for GCP Cloud Run). The new `seedDeployContext(cwd, config, changeId, opts)` function in `core/driver.js` reads the adapter name from `config.deploy.adapter`, resolves the companion `.conventions.md` file, and upserts it into `pipeline/context.md`. `loadConfig()` in `core/config.js` now exposes `config.deploy` directly (previously only available via `config._raw.deploy`). `core/deploy/README.md` documents the conventions file pattern. 6 new tests in `tests/deploy-conventions.test.js` cover the false-on-null, false-on-missing-file, write+content, idempotency, update, and auto-mkdir cases. **Phase 15 complete.** All items shipped: pre-work `devteam init --adapter` (PR #173); 15.1 gizmos auth fix / `GIZMOS_API_KEY` (PR #176); 15.2 conventions injection (this item, feat/adapter-conventions).

- **Dogfooding guide — `docs/guides/dogfooding.md` (Phase 14.5). Phase 14 complete.** New user-facing guide covering the complete dogfooding workflow: prerequisites (dedicated clone, `npm link`), one-time setup with `devteam init --profile dogfood` + `devteam doctor`, per-feature workflow (branch → `devteam run --budget-usd` → review → commit → clean up), recommended budget table ($3–25 depending on depth), infrastructure guard explanation, failure-mode table, and expected `devteam doctor` output for "Dogfood mode". Cross-referenced from `docs/user-guide.md` § What's not covered here and indexed in `docs/README.md`. `devteam --help` updated to document `--profile dogfood` on the `init` command. **Phase 14 complete.** All five items shipped: 14.1 `--profile dogfood` (PR #163); 14.2 doctor dogfood checks (PR #164); 14.3 preflight artifact guard (PR #165); 14.4 budget warning (PR #166); 14.5 this guide.

- **`devteam run` now warns on stderr when no `--budget-usd` cap is set (Phase 14.4).** When the driver starts without a budget cap (`budgetUsd === null`), it immediately writes a two-line warning to stderr: `[devteam run] Warning: no --budget-usd cap set. The run will not halt on spend.` / `Use --budget-usd <amount> to prevent runaway cost.` This appears before the first stage dispatch so operators can abort early. The warning does not affect exit codes or pipeline behavior — it is purely advisory. 2 new tests in `tests/driver.test.js`: warning present without cap, absent when cap is set.

- **`devteam preflight` now blocks PRs that contain staged pipeline artifacts (Phase 14.3).** A new check (`checkStagedPipelineArtifacts`) runs `git diff --cached --name-only` and emits a BLOCKER if any file matching the artifact prefix list (`pipeline/brief.md`, `pipeline/context.md`, `pipeline/spec.feature`, `pipeline/gates/`, `pipeline/code-review/`, `pipeline/changes/`, and nine other runtime paths) appears in the git index. Applies to all projects — staging these files into a peer-review PR is always a mistake. Handles non-git directories gracefully (returns `[]`, no error). 8 new tests in `tests/preflight.test.js` using real git tempdirs.

- **`devteam doctor` now shows a "Dogfood mode" section when `profile: dogfood` is set (Phase 14.2).** Six checks are shown: pre-commit infrastructure guard present, hook executable, `# BEGIN stagecraft-dogfood` block in `.gitignore`, `pipeline/stages/deploy.md` in `.git/info/exclude`, no `npm publish` script (warn if present — anti-pattern for dogfooding), and a budget-usd reminder (info). The section is entirely absent when the profile is not set. Profile is read from the raw parsed YAML (`config._raw?.profile`); no changes to `loadConfig()` needed. 14 new tests in `tests/doctor.test.js`.

- **`devteam init --profile dogfood` writes four dogfooding safeguards (Phase 14.1).** Running `devteam init --host <x> --profile dogfood` now writes a supplemental `# BEGIN stagecraft-dogfood` gitignore block covering generated pipeline documents (`brief.md`, `context.md`, `spec.feature`, `runbook.md`, `test-report.md`, `code-review/`, and their `pipeline/changes/*/` counterparts), installs a pre-commit hook that blocks accidental commits to `core/`, `bin/devteam`, `pipeline/stages/`, `roles/`, or `rules/`, adds `pipeline/stages/deploy.md` to `.git/info/exclude`, and writes a `profile: dogfood` marker to `.devteam/config.yml`. All writes are idempotent — re-running the command skips already-correct state.

- **Gizmos deploy adapter — Phase 13.2. Phase 13 complete.** `core/deploy/gizmos.md` is a new built-in deploy adapter for stage-08 targeting the Gizmos platform (Cloudflare Workers, `gizmos.run`). It pushes source code via `gizmos push`, smoke-tests the live `$APP.gizmos.run` URL with `curl --retry 3`, and writes a compliant stage-08 gate with `deploy_completed`, `smoke_tests_passed`, and `rollback_executed`. Config: `deploy.gizmos.app` (required; must match `wrangler.toml name`) and `deploy.gizmos.src` (required). Precondition checks: PM sign-off gate, `wrangler.toml` existence, `app`/`name` match, runbook `§Rollback` section, `gizmos whoami` auth. **Platform constraints** section documents the Cloudflare Workers runtime limits (TS/JS or Python only; no persistent filesystem; stateless `fetch()` handler; ~30s CPU per request). `core/deploy/README.md` table updated. `tests/deploy-adapters.test.js` extended with 11 new tests (README table sync, file existence, all required sections including `## Platform constraints`, three schema-required gate fields). Suite: 1793 pass (CI mode), lint clean, consistency 319 checks green.

- **GCP Cloud Run deploy adapter — Phase 13.1.** `core/deploy/cloud-run.md` is a new built-in deploy adapter for stage-08. It builds a Docker image, pushes to GCP Artifact Registry, deploys a new Cloud Run revision via `gcloud run deploy`, smoke-tests the live URL with `curl --retry 3`, records the active revision name for rollback, and writes `pipeline/deploy-log.md`. Gate body uses the three schema-required fields: `deploy_completed`, `smoke_tests_passed`, `rollback_executed`. Config: `deploy.cloud_run.project` / `.region` / `.service` (required); `.repository` (default `"cloud-run"`); `.image_tag` (default: git short SHA); `.extra_flags`. Universal fields `deploy.environment` and `deploy.smoke_test_path` introduced for all adapters. `core/deploy/README.md` table updated with the new row. New `tests/deploy-adapters.test.js` (10 tests) validates adapter-file existence, all required sections, the three schema-required gate fields, and README table sync — catches future adapter drift. Suite: 1782 pass (CI mode), lint clean, consistency 319 checks green.

- **`devteam commit` command — Phase 12.2 (ADR-010).** New first-class command that stages exactly the right pipeline artifacts for completed stages and generates a meaningful commit message — without any manual `git add` decisions from the operator. Reads `pipeline/run-state.json`, determines which stages have not yet been committed (idempotency cursor `last_committed_stage_index`), stages the gate file for each PASS/WARN stage plus named artifacts from `core/pipeline/artifacts.js` (brief.md, design-spec.md, spec.feature, code-review/, test-report.md, etc.), excludes all volatile runtime files (run-state.json, run.lock, run-log.jsonl, logs/, gates/archive/, etc.), generates `"pipeline: stages NN–NN PASS"` (or `"pipeline(repair): ..."` for repair runs), and prompts `y/n/e` before committing with a `Co-Authored-By: Stagecraft` trailer. `--all` ignores cursor; `--dry-run` prints without committing; `--message` overrides; `--json` emits machine-readable output. Calling it twice is a no-op (second call prints "nothing to commit"). `run-state.json` gains `stages_advanced` (stage IDs in pipeline order) and `last_committed_stage_index` (commit cursor, null until first commit); old run-states are migrated on read. `RUN_SCHEMA_VERSION` bumped to 1.2. Stage artifact registry in `core/pipeline/artifacts.js` covers all 19 stage IDs. Tests: 27 new tests in `tests/commit.test.js` (file selection, cursor mechanics, repair-mode diagnosis.md substitution, schema migration, volatile exclusion, WARN gate inclusion, directory and glob artifact expansion, CLI registration, and driver state writing). Honest scope note: in-place pipeline mode only (changeId=null); bounded-workspace support is Phase 12.3+. Suite: 1757 pass, lint clean, consistency 319 checks green.

- **Track provenance: `pipeline/track.json` + confirmation guard — Phase 11.3 (ADR-006). Phase 11 complete.** `devteam assess` (default) now writes `pipeline/track.json` — a per-run track inference record with `{track, source, confidence, reasons, assessed_at, assessed_by}` — so every autonomous run has a traceable provenance record. `--confirm` sets `source: "human"`; `--apply` still writes `pipeline.custom_stages` to `.devteam/config.yml` (no breaking change). `resolveTrack` in `core/driver.js` now returns `{track, source, confidence}` and reads `pipeline/track.json` in precedence order: `--track > pipeline/track.json > custom_stages > default_track > "full"`. New `run-start` event in `run-log.jsonl` carries `track_source` and `track_confidence` for audit provenance; `track-confidence-check` event logged on every guard invocation. New `autonomy.require_confirmed_track` config flag (default `false`): when on, an inferred `pipeline/track.json` at medium or low confidence halts with `unconfirmed-track` requiring `--track` or `--force`; high confidence proceeds. The flag is **not** keyed on `CI=true` (which is already overloaded by validator strict-mode and the verify runner). Docs: `docs/tracks.md` "Track record" section; `docs/runbooks/autonomous-run.md` pre-run checklist + `unconfirmed-track` halt row; `docs/ci.md` assess step + `require_confirmed_track` section. Tests: 9 new tests covering `resolveTrack` precedence, confidence guard (flag off warn-only, flag on medium/low halt, flag on high proceeds, human source no halt, `--force` bypass, run-start event fields), and CLI assess behavior. Suite: 1718 pass, lint clean, consistency green.

- **Advisory sweep on completion + `--fail-on-advisory` — Phase 11.2 (ADR-008).** After `pipeline-complete`, the driver now runs an in-process advisory sweep (reusing `core/advise.js` classification: `QA_BLOCKER` / `PEER_REVIEW_RISK` / `A11Y_FIX`) and adds `advisory_blockers_count` and `advisory_breakdown` (per-class counts) to the `--json` summary (schema version bumped to `1.1`). When any blocker-class items remain, the driver prints a loud line to stderr: `pipeline complete — N advisory blocker(s) remain; run \`devteam advise\` to review`. **The default exit code is unchanged** — `pipeline-complete` still exits 0 so external `if devteam run; then merge` consumers are unaffected (ADR-008 revision: A-default). New opt-in `--fail-on-advisory` flag exits **3** (not 1 — preserves the failed-vs-advisory distinction) when unaddressed blocker-class items remain; default threshold: `QA_BLOCKER` + `A11Y_FIX`; `--fail-on-advisory=all` adds `PEER_REVIEW_RISK`. Flag parser extended to support `--flag=value` form and a new `"toggle"` type (bare → `true`, `=value` → value string). Docs: exit-code table updated (0/1/2/3) in `docs/runbooks/autonomous-run.md` and the CLI reference; `docs/ci.md` lenient-vs-strict advisory gate examples added. Tests: 7 new tests (2 driver-level, 5 CLI-subprocess) in `tests/run.test.js`. Suite: 1709 pass, lint clean, consistency green (318 checks).

- **Liveness heartbeat + observe-only stall detector — Phase 11.1 (ADR-007 Tier 1).** The driver now emits a `heartbeat` event to `run-log.jsonl` (and via `onEvent`) at the **start of every loop iteration**, before `next()`, so the last-event age is always bounded. Alongside each `run-stage`/`continue-stage` dispatch an **observe-only stall probe** runs fire-and-forget: it wakes every 60 s and emits `stall-detected` (with `stall_class: "observed"`) if neither the workstream log (under `pipeline/logs/`) grew ≥ 512 bytes nor any gate updated within 5 minutes. The dispatch is **never killed, no `Promise.race` fires** (Tier-1 observe-only; the probe self-cancels via a `cancelled` flag when the dispatch settles, preventing stale events). Config: optional `autonomy.stall_threshold_ms` (default 300000) and `autonomy.stall_min_growth_bytes` (default 512) in `.devteam/config.yml`. The probe detects silent hangs only — loop-spew (repetitive output that resets the clock) rides with ADR-007 Tier 2. New read-only **`devteam status`** command reads `run-state.json` + tail of `run-log.jsonl` and reports `status / current_stage / last_action / iterations / cost_usd / last_heartbeat_age_ms / last_event_age_ms / stall_detected`. Docs: "No heartbeat" removed from `docs/runbooks/autonomous-run.md` Honest Limitations; replaced with a full liveness section (heartbeat, stall probe, `devteam status` fields, observe-first note); `devteam status` added to help text. Tests: 4 new tests in `tests/run.test.js` (heartbeat every iteration, stall-detected on flat log+gate with injected clock, no stale event after dispatch settles, no kill on stall) and 1 new `tests/status.test.js` file (ages from fixture log). Suite: 1702 pass, lint clean, consistency green (318 checks).

- **Repair-mode vocabulary map, runbook, and deferred metrics surface — Phase 10.4 (ADR-009 §Decision.8 + §Decision.7).** Ships the residual "teaching" cost named in ADR-009 §Consequences plus the advisory metrics surface. (1) **Vocabulary map** (`docs/conventions.md` § Repair-mode vocabulary): authoritative table distinguishing `--repair` (intent), `hotfix` (depth), `fix-and-retry` / `fix_steps` (internal driver machinery), and PATCH MODE (mechanism). Explains why the internal terms were not renamed and why a `--repair` run coexisting with `fix-retry` events is expected, not a bug. (2) **Repair-flow runbook** (`docs/runbooks/repair-flow.md`): covers all three operator decision points — (a) diagnosis gate approval: what to read, interactive vs autonomous approval, `--repair-at` escape hatch, and the knowledge-gate honest limitation; (b) scope-gate FAIL recovery: why it fires, how to amend the diagnosis scope and re-run, and when the gate is inert; (c) tri-state reproduction: what `reproduced: true / false / "unverifiable: <reason>"` each means, accepted skip reasons, and why repair-on-hotfix now includes stage-03b. Indexed in `docs/runbooks/README.md` (3 symptom rows + listing) and `docs/README.md`. (3) **Advisory metrics surface** (`scripts/dashboard.js` / `routing-suggest.js` / `budget.js`): `dashboard.js` accepts `--by intent` as a new grouping slice for both pass-rate and cost views; exports `computeScopeAdherence(gates)` (headline repair metric: did the build stay within the diagnosed files, from gates with `scope_adhered` field) and `computeCostInversionEstimate(...)` (savings estimate with exposed inputs — `savings ≈ diagnosisRejectionRate × avgFullBuildCost − diagnosisCost` — never a measured figure); `routing-suggest.js` adds `--intent repair|feature` filter backed by `filterByIntent(gates, intent)` so routing suggestions can be computed per-intent; `budget.js` adds `--intent` on `init`, writes an optional `Intent:` line in `pipeline/budget.md`, and preserves it through `update` calls. All three changes are advisory-only and non-blocking. `npm run docs:generate` confirmed idempotent; `--repair` flag was already in `docs/reference/cli.md` from 10.1. (4) `examples/sms-opt-in` remains the feature-intent canonical example; no separate repair example was added (none requested).

  Tests (15 new in `tests/repair-metrics.test.js`): intent slice in `aggregate()` and `aggregateCost()` (groups repair/feature gates, fallback "(no intent)"); `computeScopeAdherence` (rate computation, null when no data, feature gates excluded); `computeCostInversionEstimate` (positive savings, negative savings, missing inputs → null); `routing-suggest.filterByIntent` (repair-only, feature-only, null passthrough, exclusion of intent-less gates). Suite: 1691 pass (was 1676), 0 fail, lint clean, consistency 318 checks passed.

  **Phase 10 complete.** All four items shipped: 10.1 PATCH-MODE intent flag (PR #140); 10.2 diagnosis stage + scope gate (PR #141); 10.3 failing-first reproduction (PR #146); 10.4 vocabulary + runbook + metrics (PR #147).

- **Failing-first reproduction via stage-03b + stamp, tri-state honest skip — Phase 10.3 (ADR-009 Phase 3).** The mitigation for 10.2's knowledge-gate limit: repair runs now route through stage-03b (executable-spec) even on hotfix depth (which normally skips it), adding failing-first reproduction discipline to every repair. Concretely: (1) **Stage order injection** — for `intent === "repair"`, the driver's stage order always injects `"executable-spec"` immediately before `"build"`, regardless of the base track; hotfix repair runs gain `[requirements, executable-spec, build, ...]` instead of `[requirements, build, ...]`. (2) **`repairOverride` for stage-03b** — `core/pipeline/stages.js` adds a `repairOverride` to the `"executable-spec"` stage (same pattern as the stage-01 diagnosis override from Phase 2): in repair mode `buildDescriptor` swaps the objective to failing-first framing ("write a Gherkin Scenario that is RED before the fix and GREEN after") and adds `reproduced: false` to the gate shape so the PM model knows to set it. (3) **Tri-state `reproduced` field** — `core/gates/schemas/stage-03b.schema.json` gains an optional `reproduced` property (not in `required`; omitted for feature runs): `true` = bug reproduced and fix verified green; `false` = could not reproduce; `"unverifiable: <reason>"` = automated test impossible (external API / nondeterminism / data dependency). (4) **`stampStage03b` handles the tri-state**: `"unverifiable: ..."` → WARN loudly to `gate.warnings`, no blocker (run proceeds, but manual verification is flagged); `true|false` → runs the project's test command to capture the pre-build (pre-fix) baseline in `_orchestrator_stamped.runs.reproduction_pre_build`, marks the field as `orchestrator_deferred` pending stage-04a. (5) **`stampStage04a` finalizes `reproduced`**: after running the post-build test command, reads the stage-03b gate (if present) and writes `reproduced: true` when tests pass (green after fix confirmed), `reproduced: false` when tests fail (fix didn't work); unverifiable bugs are not touched; the `reproduction_verification` audit record (`red_before_confirmed`, `green_after_confirmed`, `post_build_tests_passed`, `pre_build_tests_passed`) is appended to `_orchestrator_stamped.runs`. Feature runs (no `reproduced` in stage-03b gate) are silently skipped. (6) The `--repair-at` escape hatch also includes stage-03b — reproduction discipline applies even when diagnosis is bypassed.

  Tests (15 new): `tests/repair-mode.test.js` section 8 — hotfix baseline confirms executable-spec absent from hotfix track; repair intent on hotfix includes executable-spec in captured order array; executable-spec placed immediately before build; full-track repair no double-inject; `--repair-at` still includes executable-spec; `buildDescriptor` repair objective mentions failing-first; feature mode gate has no `reproduced`. `tests/verify-stamp.test.js` — unverifiable adds loud WARN, no blocker; `reproduced:true` records pre-build red; `reproduced:false` records pre-build green; feature run no reproduction_pre_build; red-before/green-after stamps `reproduced:true` from stage-04a; post-fix fail stamps `reproduced:false`; unverifiable not changed by stage-04a; feature run (no stage-03b gate) skips gracefully. Suite: 1676 pass (was 1661), 0 fail, lint clean, consistency 317 checks passed.

  Honest scope note: The "red before" phase captures pre-build test state at stage-03b time; since the regression test code is written by the build agent (not the PM), the pre-build run records whatever tests already exist rather than a regression-specific test. The "green after" phase is orchestrator-verified at stage-04a via the actual test command exit code. The combination — stamp records both phases, final `reproduced` is set by the orchestrator (not the agent alone) — satisfies the "not asserted by the agent" requirement.

- **Diagnosis as fix-aware stage-01 and `--repair-at` escape hatch — Phase 10.2 (ADR-009 Phase 2).** When `intent === "repair"`, stage-01 (requirements) produces a **DIAGNOSIS** document instead of a feature brief — same stage id, same gate file path, fix-aware artifact, no new stages. Concretely: (1) `repairOverride` field added to the `requirements` stage definition; `buildDescriptor` in the orchestrator applies it when `intent === "repair"`, swapping objective, artifact (`pipeline/diagnosis.md`), template (`templates/diagnosis-template.md`), `allowedWrites`, and gate schema. (2) The diagnosis gate is always **ESCALATE-shaped** — a judgment gate with `escalation_reason` and `decision_needed` fields, requiring human approval or `--auto-rule diagnosis-approved` before the build proceeds. `diagnosis-approved` is the new ruling class slug; grant it with `--auto-rule diagnosis-approved`. (3) Driver: `repairNeedsDiagnosis = intent === "repair" && !repairAt` prepends `"requirements"` to the effective stage order so `next()` routes through diagnosis before build; the diagnosis gate's `affected_files` list is read before each build dispatch and populates `state.affectedFiles`, **activating 10.1's structural scope gate** (previously inert — no diagnosis stage existed to supply the list); `repairPatchItems` upgrades from the raw symptom string to structured per-file entries from the diagnosis. (4) **`--repair-at <file>:<line>` escape hatch** — when the caller already knows the defect location, comma-separated `file:line` locations seed `state.affectedFiles` directly, write a synthetic PASS stage-01 gate, and skip the LLM diagnosis dispatch entirely; a `repair-at-seeded` event is logged. (5) `intent` propagated through `ctx` in `runStage()` so adapters render repair-mode prompts. Tests (6 new in `tests/repair-mode.test.js`, section 7): `buildDescriptor` repair shape (unit); `buildDescriptor` feature shape unchanged (unit); scope gate activates from diagnosis gate `affected_files` without `opts.affectedFiles` (this test failed on main before 10.2); diagnosis ESCALATE halts without `--auto-rule`; `--auto-rule diagnosis-approved` proceeds; `--repair-at` escape hatch seeds, writes synthetic gate, skips dispatch. Suite: 1661 pass (was 1655), lint clean, consistency 317 checks passed.

- **`--repair "<symptom>"` intent flag — Phase 10.1 (ADR-009 Phase 1).** Adds `--repair` to `devteam run` as an intent flag orthogonal to `--track`, implemented as fix-aware wiring on existing stages — zero new stages, zero parallel pipeline. Concretely: (1) `--repair` and `--feature` are mutually exclusive (enforced in both the CLI command and the driver); (2) `--repair` defaults to `hotfix` depth; `--repair --track full` overrides; auth/payments/migration symptoms are upgraded to `full` via the same stoplist patterns (`checkStoplist`) regardless of the default — the existing lighter-track stoplist bypass does not apply here; (3) `intent: "repair" | "feature"` is written to `run-state.json` and every run-log event base object from day one (ADR-009 §Decision.7 perishable baseline — without this, repair vs feature runs are indistinguishable in history); `repair: <symptom>` string is also persisted in run-state; (4) on `--resume`, a `prior_run_id` correlation field is written linking the re-run to its predecessor's `started_at`; (5) `changeIdFromSymptom` exported from `core/config.js` (same slug algorithm as `changeIdFromFeature`) so bounded-isolation paths can derive the changeId from a symptom string (ADR-009 §Consequences); (6) repair builds dispatch `runStageHeadless` with `patchItems` set to the symptom string, activating `renderPatchBlock`'s `## ⚠️  PATCH MODE — targeted fix only` block in the build prompt — no new prompt authored (ADR-009 §Decision.2, PATCH MODE is the existing mechanism); (7) structural scope gate wired behind `state.affectedFiles` / `opts.affectedFiles`: a post-build `checkScopeGate` call (injectable for tests; default: `git diff --name-only HEAD`) fails the run with `halt_action: "scope-gate"` when files outside the list were touched. The list is supplied by 10.2's diagnosis stage (same release). CLI reference regenerated (`npm run docs:generate`). Tests (14 new in `tests/repair-mode.test.js`): mutual exclusion (both CLI and driver-level); hotfix default; `--track full` override; auth-symptom stoplist upgrade with event emission and run-log verification; intent tag in run-state and run-log; feature run intent tag; resume prior_run_id correlation; repair build patchItems dispatch; feature build lacks patchItems; `renderPatchBlock` unit check with symptom text; scope gate FAIL with synthetic affected-files list; scope gate PASS within scope; scope gate inert without affectedFiles. Suite: 1655 pass (was 1641), lint clean, consistency 317 checks passed.

- Require Phase 1 and Phase 2 audit findings to include `verified_by` evidence before they can be promoted above LOW confidence.

- Stream headless stage transcripts directly to disk so long-running host output stays observable and no longer grows Stagecraft's memory with transcript size.

- Reconcile current documentation and maintenance guidance with shipped Windows support, canonical role-tool ownership, performance-gate vocabulary, bounded autonomy, and supported embedding providers.

- Demote unused `adapterPath` and verify runner timeout exports to private module details.

type: feat

- Add `gizmos` deploy adapter (`core/deploy/gizmos.md`): pushes source to the Gizmos platform (Cloudflare Workers, gizmos.run) via `gizmos push`, smoke-tests the live URL, and writes a compliant stage-08 gate with `deploy_completed`, `smoke_tests_passed`, and `rollback_executed` fields.

- **docs(analysis):** D5 adaptive routing evidence review (`plans/adaptive-routing-evidence.md`). Runs `devteam routing:suggest` against all accumulated pipeline gate data; finds zero real-run telemetry — only the hand-authored `examples/sms-opt-in` fixture (max 4 dispatches per role, no cost data). The tool's MIN_DISPATCHES guard fires for all 6 roles; no recommendations are produced; human conclusion matches tool output. Verdict: continuous adaptive routing stays gated pending ≥5 dispatches per (role, host) pair across ≥2 real projects, cost telemetry, and ADR-007 implementation. Honest scope note: ADR-007 precondition not yet met.

- **docs(adr):** Draft ADR-007 — Liveness/heartbeat (`docs/adr/007-liveness-heartbeat.md`, Status: Proposed). Defines *stall* (host process alive, output flowing, no gate progress) as distinct from the wall-clock `timeoutMs` timeout; specifies a per-iteration `heartbeat` event in `run-log.jsonl` and a dispatch-progress probe (log-file growth vs gate mtime) running as `Promise.race` alongside each dispatch; specifies operator surface (`devteam status` reporting `last_heartbeat_age_ms` and `stall_detected`, and a `devteam run --watch` rendering flag); stall response reuses the existing `transient`/`structural` vocabulary from `classifyDispatch`. Ends with 7 open questions for human ruling. Updates `docs/adr/README.md` to move ADR-007 from Deferred to the main Proposed index.

- **docs(adr):** Draft ADR-008 — Exit semantics (`docs/adr/008-exit-semantics.md`, Status: Proposed). Confirms [verify-first]: `devteam run` exits 0 on `pipeline-complete` with pending advise blockers — the design-doc step 9 ("run a final advise sweep and exit") was never implemented. ADR frames four options (status quo, exit 1, exit 3, opt-in flag), with exit 3 as the recommended candidate for evaluation; ends with 4 open questions for human ruling including severity threshold and CI contract documentation. Updates `docs/adr/README.md` to move ADR-008 from Deferred to the main Proposed index.

- **Docs: FAQ entry on preserving implementation constraints through the PM interpretation layer.** The PM agent rewrites `--feature` as product intent, which can normalise away specific env var names, API endpoints, or explicit exclusions. The new entry covers: (1) phrasing constraints as observable behaviours so the PM captures them as ACs, (2) seeding `pipeline/context.md` with a binding constraints block before running, and (3) how to open a build-gate blocker and re-run after the build has already produced the wrong implementation.

## `docs/git-workflow.md` restructure + repair-mode commit guidance (Phase 12.4)

**`docs/git-workflow.md`** restructured into a 7-section outline covering both operator
modes: §1 Overview (interactive vs. autonomous; what lives in git), §2 What to commit vs.
ignore (reference `devteam init` gitignore block; include/exclude table), §3 Interactive
mode (`devteam commit` as primary interface; per-stage-group commit cadence; Stage 04
worktrees), §4 Autonomous mode (new: `halt_action` table identifying `ceiling`/`until`/
`budget` as clean commit signals; `devteam commit` after a halt; `--auto-commit` opt-in
semantics), §5 Repair mode (new: three natural commit points; note that source file staging
is the operator's responsibility), §6 PR timing, §7 CI integration.

**`docs/runbooks/repair-flow.md`** — added "When to commit in repair mode" section: three
commit points (after diagnosis gate approval, after failing-first test, after build + scope
gate PASS). Notes that `devteam commit` handles gate files automatically; application source
files require explicit `git add` by the operator.

**Phase 12 complete.** All four items shipped: managed gitignore (PR #154), `devteam commit`
(PR #155), `--auto-commit` (PR #156), docs restructure (PR #157).
